# V16 temporal reconstruction: six-run results

**Operational recovery disclosure.** Five completed training trajectories and their artifacts remain unchanged. Candidate seed946103 was externally interrupted after its persisted typed checkpoint512; its original CSVs contain complete rows through553 and565 plus torn next rows. The separately admitted recovery executable restored512 and produced logical updates513–2048. Every available replayed complete CSV row and torn fragment prefix matched exactly before canonical reconciliation. This is a recovered execution under the [additive operational amendment](../cpb_v16_temporal_recovery_v1/RECOVERY.md). The exact physical repeated-update count, complete original execution cost and whole-trajectory per-parameter counters are unknown. No uninterrupted-process or zero-recomputation claim is made.

Execution is **complete and valid** for all six declared trajectories and their development/confirmation arithmetic audits. The saved prospective screen verdict is **mixed**; the declared promising rule does not pass. Work completion and scientific success are separate judgments. No encoder is automatically promoted or selected for Mityane.

This document formats the [authoritative machine report](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/report.json). Values are displayed to six decimal places; full-precision JSON, original predicate objects, undefined metrics and individual repetitions remain authoritative. This formatter computes no metrics, paired differences, medians, intervals, rankings or scientific verdicts. It copies saved values and flags.

The frozen machine reporter and machine report remain unchanged. Their recovered candidate training summary and phase dependency graph carry the operational extension; the mandatory [recovered-report verification sidecar](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/recovery-report-verification-001.json) binds these exact report bytes to the actual new recovery source, executable, admission and publication. A fresh recovery guard passed before this formatting step.

## Methods

This is an additive, fresh controlled native36 study. Each seed946101/946102/946103 has one newly initialized V4 control and one newly initialized V16 candidate. Neither arm imports trained weights, old fitted preprocessing or prior head fits. Native36 is learned20 + fixed spectral12 + mean4: 293672 learned values and3888 fixed values, 297560 total across76 parameter tensors. These controls are new matched blocks; they are distinct from the historical single V4 leader and the completed V15 controls.

Control trains with the original V4 value Gaussian NLL. Candidate trains with **V4 NLL +0.1 × (L1+L4+L16)/3**. For each raw minute-row lag1/4/16, the residual is `((mu[t]-mu[t-lag])-(target[t]-target[t-lag]))/sqrt(2)`. Both endpoints must be in the original observed-and-hidden target mask Q, within the same channel/feature and retained128 positions. There is no wrap, compressed observed indexing or target expansion from context deletion. Endpoints are sanitized under pair support and promoted to F64 before subtraction. Each L uses Huberδ1 and pair→eligible-channel→eligible-example means. Empty lags contribute connected zero with their fixed one-third weight; active lags are never renormalized. No new target scaler or parameter is added.

Gaussian supervision and the detached variance-head input are retained. The auxiliary directly supervises the mean path; it does not add variance supervision. One AdamW and one shared global clip1 still couple update scaling. This does not establish optimization independence. Reported Gaussian NLL is a reconstruction score, distinct from the composite candidate training objective.

Each trajectory completes2048 successful B8 updates, at most4096 attempts, AdamW lr0.001/weight_decay0.0001, mask25%, context deletion15%, dropout0, and an actual exact typed resume at512. Row/mask/context seeds are encoder+1000/+2000/+3000, paired by declaration. Initial0 and final2048 checkpoints receive saved-checkpoint causal proofs; final2048 is the sole outcome checkpoint. No rescue seed, mask, coefficient, checkpoint or head is selected.

For candidate946103,2048 is the accepted logical trajectory length. Recovery repeated discarded physical work after512; its exact amount is unknown. The original in-process typed512 proof remains original evidence, and the later external recovery has separate source, binary, validation and execution identities. The first-update witnesses remain original bytes; the final-update2048 witnesses were produced during recovery.

January2024 BTCUSDT/ETHUSDT/SOLUSDT remains repeated development evidence: days1–21 TRAIN, days22–28 known validation,4096 calibration anchors and112/32 probes, recent128 from H256 legal support. Encoder reads genuine F64 CACHE88; targets preserve the legacy F32-loader→F64 route with bitwise January parity. All preprocessing and heads are freshly TRAIN-fitted. Market heads retain ridge1 and joint four-output tanh16/Adam0.01/100 steps. Reps2701/2802/2903 use exact D36 uint64 seeds `13088411117524848790`, `10018485049408248344`, `9083751155097190455`. TEMPO retains masters941101/941102/941103,512 TRAIN/256 validation, both TRAIN-fitted input-scaler policies and intact/30%-deleted views.

March2024 is the newly declared confirmation period; February was already consumed by V15. The declared three assets each contain44640 minute rows, with32 timestamp-only midpoint probes and disjoint H256 legal support, H128 targets, and unchanged32 Boolean query geometries. All six training trajectories complete before development extraction and head fitting. Fixed-query scores captured during training are retained without tuning. Final checkpoints, original TRAIN-fitted heads and six development audits are sealed before March features/predictions/outcomes are opened. Confirmation performs no fit, calibration, row replacement or selection. Its query MAE/NLL is descriptive and has no gate.

The four tasks are past-window readouts, not forecasts. Linear scores and neural means are copied separately; each neural mean summarizes three head repetitions of one encoder. Initial scores use separately fitted heads under the same frozen recipe.

### Fixed-query lag16 is unsupported

The unchanged query hides one eight-step patch per eligible channel. It contains no pair of originally hidden endpoints16 minutes apart. Its lag16 diagnostic is **unsupported with null loss**, not a zero-error score. This differs from the graph-connected zero retained with weight1/3 when a training lag has no pairs. ## Temporal training arithmetic and support

Candidate witnesses at actual updates1 and2048 are independently checked against their same-step original objective witnesses. The scalar auditor reconstructs original-Q pair support, hierarchical losses and total direct mean/variance output adjoints, then compares actual retained post-backward **preclip F32 gradients**. Tolerances are absolute2e-7/relative2e-5 for these output gradients; the existing F64 arithmetic tolerances remain separate. It does not reconstruct encoder Jacobians, full parameter gradients, global clipping or AdamW trajectories. Whole training-log bytes are bound; intervening2046 updates are not independently reconstructed.

## Engineering control history

The [original engineering001 log](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/engineering-001.log) remains failed/incomplete: its V16 variance fixture stopped at a stale copied V4 gradient-policy assertion before variance arithmetic. The [first fixture repair](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/variance-fixture-repair-001.json) changes only that expected literal. Engineering002 passed the corrected V16 variance check, then stopped at the public eval/GradMode guard in its temporal fixture; its [log](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/engineering-002.log) remains failed/incomplete. The [second fixture repair](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/temporal-fixture-repair-001.json) puts the paired test models in training mode with dropout0 for their required backward pass. Both attempts retain their failure logs and markers. The [reviewed continuation compatibility](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/engineering-continuation-compatibility004.json) records all three source/build generations and retained original source/binary identities.

Engineering003 was refused during metadata preflight because the shared live Makefile had changed; its [retained refusal record](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/engineering-preflight-refusal003.json) records zero model/CUDA calls and no phase output directory or log. The [context-only preservation review](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/build-context-freeze-review002.json) binds the exact build003 Makefile bytes at a distinct frozen locator. Source006 uses that explicit locator while all production, runner, fixture and configuration source identities remain unchanged from005. Build004 associates the same seventeen completed build003 binaries; it is not a compiler replay. The historical original path and current retained path remain distinct; the live Makefile observation is time-qualified and is not a freshness input for this study.

The passed full-model fixtures and V4 variance check from001, plus the passed V16 variance check from002, were reused under exact unchanged executable identities without repetition. Only the corrected temporal mechanism, native/Node audit fixtures and final GPU inventory executed in engineering004; the [actual continuation execution](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/engineering-execution-005.json) and [passed engineering review](../../../../../.temp/embeddings/runs/cpb-v16-temporal-001/attempt-001/engineering-review-005.json) record those results. The production objective, settings and scientific runtime remain unchanged. January parity and blind March target preparation retain their actual003 evidence and unchanged target-preparation binary, without replay or relabeling as the later source generation. No scientific trajectory was replayed for either engineering fixture repair or the context-only remap.

## Limits and historical context

March has now been inspected for this declared study and is consumed evidence for later recipe choices. January remains development evidence; no historical score is replayed or replaced. A paired point difference does not identify a general causal mechanism or establish forecasting or downstream utility. Fresh V4-control observations are distinct from candidate effects.

- Three paired encoder seeds on one additional month constitute a screen, not general-purpose superiority.
- January was repeatedly used and remains development evidence; March comparisons are within the new paired arms.
- February was already consumed by V15 and is not fresh confirmation for V16; March becomes observed evidence after this declared study.
- Head repetitions are not independent encoder or market-population replications.
- Saved-fit arithmetic is independently reconstructed; encoder forwards and optimizer trajectories are not independently reimplemented.
- Temporal witnesses audit only updates1/2048 and direct output adjoints, not the full encoder Jacobian, every remaining training update or full global-clip norm.
- Value/temporal direct routes share one global clip1 and AdamW; objective components are not optimization-independent.
- Training fixed-query waveform witnesses cover first8 examples only; full112/32 saved row reductions are checked without claiming full waveform reconstruction.
- Conditional bootstrap intervals from the frozen producer are retained but are not independently audited.
- Past-window readout, reconstruction and TEMPO do not establish forecasting, interventions or downstream decision utility.

The final reporting barrier coalesces only complete identical file tuples: 20100 declared file-pin occurrences receive 2048 fresh distinct file checks. Initial and per-read barriers remain fresh and separate. Exact duplicate checks are coalesced within this final barrier on completed sealed artifacts; this is not observationally identical to repeated checks with concurrent writers. Initial and per-read checks remain separate and fresh; unique final files retain unchanged canonical, single-link and full-hash checks.

The machine report, study card and development seal retain exact source, protocol, settings, data, environment, checkpoints, TRAIN-fitted heads, receipts and binary identities. Original historical evidence keeps its repository/path/bytes/hash identity and an explicit current locator. Saved-metric formatting decodes no tensor payload and executes no model, optimizer or fit. Its mandatory recovery guard separately freshly hashes retained artifacts and binaries, verifies the actual new recovery call and publication graph, and recomputes exact metadata CSV reconciliation. Raw data and checkpoints remain ignored local artifacts. No publication, catalog application, overall-winner claim or Mityane selection follows from this document.
